# 1402 new apps — 2026-11-23

Window: apps first found between 2026-11-22 18:46 UTC (19:46 Europe/Warsaw) and 2026-11-23 21:23 UTC (22:23 Europe/Warsaw) and released since 2026-11-21 18:46 UTC (19:46 Europe/Warsaw), plus apps found earlier whose release date arrived in that span. Date-only release dates count by calendar day in America/Los_Angeles. Storefronts: us.
